# Trendjack make-and-sell playbooks

Generated 2026-10-04 from the research and playbooks in this repository. The same content, with copy buttons and an editable price, is in the dashboard under each product.

Playbooks are guidance, not evidence. Prices and POD costs are estimates unless the dashboard marks them verified; fees use `config/fees` (verified 2026-10-04) where available. Per-sale arithmetic only, never a sales forecast.

## Tarot Study Journal for Beginners: 78-Card Notes & Spread Log

**Trend:** Cosmic Reset (beginner astrology & tarot) (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (89/100)

**Buyer:** Tarot beginners

### Super prompt

```text
You are a patient tarot teacher and a book designer. Your goal: write the complete text and page plan for a beginner tarot STUDY journal: a log for learning one's own deck, not an interpretation book.

BUYER AND JOB
Tarot beginners with a new deck (they search "tarot cards for beginners"). Job: one page per card to record what they learn from their own deck and guidebook, a spread log, and weekly pages for noticing patterns.

DELIVERABLE: 150 pages, number every page
1 Title: "[PRODUCT TITLE]", subtitle "A study journal for learning all 78 cards", "[YOUR SHOP NAME]".
2 How to Use: one card a day (78 days) or one suit at a time; use your own deck and guidebook; log every practice spread.
3 How a Tarot Deck Is Organized: 78 cards; 22 Major Arcana numbered 0-21; 56 Minor Arcana in four suits of 14 (Ace to 10, Page, Knight, Queen, King); some decks rename suits (Coins or Disks for Pentacles) or court cards; Strength and Justice are numbered 8 and 11 in some traditions and 11 and 8 in others, so follow your deck.
4 Suits and Elements: the most common modern pairing is Wands-Fire, Cups-Water, Swords-Air, Pentacles-Earth; some readers swap Wands and Swords. Table plus "My deck uses: ______".
5 My Decks: deck name, date acquired, first impressions (3 blocks).
6 About Reversals: an optional practice; some readers use them and some don't; "My approach: ______".
7-8 78-Card Checklist: every card name with three boxes (Studied | Pulled in a spread | Revisited).
9-86 One page per card, in this order: 0 The Fool, I The Magician, II The High Priestess, III The Empress, IV The Emperor, V The Hierophant, VI The Lovers, VII The Chariot, VIII Strength, IX The Hermit, X Wheel of Fortune, XI Justice, XII The Hanged Man, XIII Death, XIV Temperance, XV The Devil, XVI The Tower, XVII The Star, XVIII The Moon, XIX The Sun, XX Judgement, XXI The World; then Wands, Cups, Swords and Pentacles, each Ace, Two to Ten, Page, Knight, Queen, King. Card page fields: header; Date first studied; What I see in my deck's image (colors, figures, symbols, mood); Keywords from my guidebook; My own keywords; Upright notes; Reversed notes (optional); Questions I have; "Came up in" log (date and spread, 8 rows).
87-122 Spread Log (36 pages): date; deck; spread name; question or focus; sketch box for the layout; table Position | Card | What I noticed (10 rows); Overall reflection; Looking back a week later.
123-124 Spread Templates: a 3-card layout with blank position labels and example label sets (past / present / next step; situation / challenge / next step); a 10-position layout with numbered positions only.
125-146 Weekly "What I Noticed" pages (22): suits that showed up most, cards I keep drawing, a card that confused me, what I want to study next.
147-150 Notes and "Cards I Want to Revisit".

DESIGN
Two outputs from the same content. Paperback interior: 6x9 in, no bleed, 0.5 in margins on all sides, black ink. Printable: US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4. Headings Cinzel, body Lora 10.5 pt. Palette "Candle & Ink" (cover and printable accents): Ink #1E1B22, Bone #F3EEE3, Oxblood #7A2E35, Antique Gold #B8975A, Smoke #8C8790. Writing lines 50% gray. No card images inside.

CONTENT RULES
Original text for pages 2-6 and all prompts; friendly, practical, about 8th-grade level. Do NOT write meanings or interpretations for any card. Describe tarot as a reflective practice people approach in different ways.

WHY THIS IS BETTER
A study log, not another meanings book: it works with any deck, records what the reader actually learned, and pairs with a learn-astrology workbook.

CONSTRAINTS
No predictions, and no promises of insight, luck, love or success. No deck, publisher, artist or author names. No guidebook text. No reproduction or description of any copyrighted card artwork.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Page by page: "=== PAGE [n]: [title] ===", "Layout:" (one line), then exact text. For pages 9-86 give ONE card-page template, then a numbered list of the 78 headers in order. Do the same for spread-log and weekly pages (one template plus the page range). End with:
SELF-CHECK (fix any "no"): 78 headers = 22 Major + 4 x 14 Minor; no card meanings anywhere; page total is 150; no brand or person names; no predictions; wording original.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Cover art (printable cover and KDP front cover); Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Original cover design inspired by an ornate card back: a tall arched frame filled with a symmetrical pattern of a central eight-pointed star, a sun disc above, a crescent moon below and fine radiating lines. Engraved-line style, antique gold #B8975A linework on ink black #1E1B22 with a few oxblood #7A2E35 accents. Empty band at the top for a title added in Canva. Aspect ratio 2:3 portrait (at least 1800x2700 px). Solid background. Text: none. No figures or scenes from any existing tarot deck, no imitation of any famous deck's style, no reproduced card art. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Suit icons for page headers and dividers; ChatGPT images or Ideogram):**

```text
Set of four simple, original line icons in a row: a wooden wand with two small leaves, a stemmed cup, an upright sword, and a coin with a five-pointed star. Single-weight lines in ink #1E1B22, no fill, flat and minimal. Transparent background (or solid white to remove in Canva). Aspect ratio 4:1. Text: none. Not copied from any deck's symbols. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Mockup background for listing photos; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Overhead photo of an open journal with plain cream pages (nothing written) on dark slate linen, with a lit taper candle, dried rose petals and a small brass dish. Warm low light; the journal centered and flat so real pages can be placed over it in Canva. Aspect ratio 4:5. Text: none. No tarot cards visible, no hands. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Paste the super prompt into Claude or ChatGPT; continue until all 150 pages are specified. Check the 78 headers against the self-check and confirm there are no card meanings.
2. In Canva, create a 6 x 9 in design with 0.5 in margin guides on all sides. Build masters for the card page, spread log and weekly page; duplicate the card master 78 times and paste the headers in order.
3. Export the KDP interior: Download > PDF Print, no crop marks; confirm 150 pages.
4. KDP cover: in KDP's cover calculator choose paperback, 6x9, white paper, black ink, 150 pages. Make a Canva design at the full-wrap size it gives (bleed included), put the art on the front and a short description on the back, leave the barcode area empty, and add spine text only if the calculator allows it at this page count. Export PDF Print.
5. KDP setup: title and subtitle with no deck or brand names, answer the AI-generated content question honestly, choose a free KDP ISBN, set the price, and order one proof copy to check margins.
6. Printable: copy the pages into US Letter and A4 designs (scale the layout up, keep 0.5 in margins) and export PDF Print (300 DPI).
7. Etsy: one standalone listing, plus one bundle listing that includes the astrology workbook files, priced below the two bought separately. Add both to Gumroad.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my printable "[PRODUCT TITLE]": a 150-page tarot study journal for beginners with a notes page for each of the 78 cards, a 78-card checklist, 36 spread-log pages, spread templates and weekly "what I noticed" pages. It contains no card meanings and no card art, so it works with any 78-card deck. Files: US Letter PDF and A4 PDF.

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase (for example "Tarot Journal for Beginners" or "Tarot Study Journal"), then key features. Give 3 options and mark your pick.
2. 13 TAGS, each 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, covering tarot journal, learn tarot, tarot for beginners, spread log, tarot notebook, printable and gift.
3. DESCRIPTION: two-line opener; "What's inside" bullets; "Files and sizes"; "How delivery works" (instant download from Etsy Purchases; nothing ships; print all pages or only the ones you need); "Good to know" (a study log, not a meanings book; use it with your own deck and guidebook); one line saying AI tools helped create it and I edited and checked it; personal-use terms.
4. FAQ with 5 Q&As: Does it include card meanings? Will it work with my deck? Can I print only some pages? Is this a reading service? (No: it is a journal for your own study.) Refunds (answer: [YOUR REFUND POLICY]).

Rules: original wording; no deck, publisher, artist or author names, including the names of famous historical or commercial decks; no predictions; no promises of insight, luck, love or success; no "best" claims; plain US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): The printable sells standalone and in a bundle with the astrology workbook, which is the input's validation test.
- **Amazon KDP** (secondary): A 6x9 paperback reaches gift buyers on Amazon with no inventory, and it is the pivot route if the bundle doesn't lift sales.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Bundle add-on for the workbook's email list.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Beginner tarot is a long-running Pinterest topic; study-page pins can link to Etsy or the Amazon page.

**Positioning:** Not another meanings book: a study log for learning your own deck one card at a time, with a matching astrology workbook.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Nov 16, 2026):** Run the super prompt and build the 6x9 interior in Canva. (Run this after the astrology workbook's go decision; if the workbook stopped, test the journal standalone.)
- **Day 2 (Wed Nov 18):** Build the Letter and A4 printables, the cover and the mockups.
- **Day 4 (Fri Nov 20):** List on Etsy standalone and as an "Astrology Workbook + Tarot Study Journal" bundle; add it to Gumroad and email the workbook list.
- **Day 5 (Sat Nov 21):** Upload the paperback to KDP (review can take up to 72 hours) and order one proof copy.
- **Day 7 (Mon Nov 23):** Start a short-video series, "learning one card a day": film your handwriting on the journal page rather than showing commercial deck art.
- **Days 5-25 (Nov 21-Dec 11):** Publish 3 pins a week: "how a tarot deck is organized", card-page close-ups, the spread log.
- **Day 14 (Mon Nov 30):** Compare conversion (orders per visit) for the workbook alone vs the bundle in Etsy Stats; log the attach rate.
- **Day 28 (Mon Dec 14):** GO / PIVOT / STOP: continue if the bundle raises conversion. Pivot to the standalone KDP paperback as a holiday gift (point pins to the Amazon page) if there is no lift. Stop if there is no lift and no standalone sales after 4 weeks.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Pins of the deck-structure page, card-page template and spread log, linking to Etsy (later also the Amazon page). | 3 pins a week |
| TikTok / Instagram Reels | "One card a day" study clips showing handwriting in the journal, with no commercial deck art on screen. | 3 short videos a week during the test |
| Tarot-learning subreddits and Discords | Share the free "how a deck is organized" page where allowed; check the community rules before posting any link. | 1-2 posts a month where allowed |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** tarot journal, tarot study journal, tarot for beginners, learn tarot, tarot notebook, tarot spread journal, tarot log book, tarot workbook, tarot gift

**Hooks:**
- I'm learning all 78 tarot cards with my own deck. Day 1: The Fool.
- A tarot journal with no meanings in it, on purpose.
- 78 cards, 78 pages, one study log.
- What I noticed after 30 practice spreads (from my spread log).

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $6.00 (range $4.00–$9.00, estimate: tarot journal prices were not measured in the research). A template-style journal sells as an add-on, so keep the printable cheap enough to lift a bundle; price the 6x9 KDP paperback at about $9.99 for gifting.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $6.00 | $1.02 | $0.00 | **$4.98** | 83% |
| Amazon KDP (120-page B&W, 50% royalty) | $6.00 | $3.00 | $2.44 | **$0.56** | 9% |
| Gumroad | $6.00 | $1.10 | $0.00 | **$4.90** | 82% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 10 hours; answer in about 28 days.
- **What you risk:** About 10 hours, $0.20 per Etsy listing and roughly $5-$10 for a KDP proof copy (estimate). You get an answer within 4 weeks of listing.
- **What stays open:** One interior serves three formats (printable, bundle add-on, KDP paperback with Amazon's own traffic). Not tied to the New Year, and the digital version has zero marginal cost.
- **Bad bet if:** Tarot journals have been sold on Etsy and KDP for years (likely high competition, not measured), a study log is easy to copy, and if the astrology workbook is stopped the bundle test can't run.

**Ways to extend:** 6x9 paperback gift edition on KDP; Bundle with the astrology workbook and the moon planner; Undated 'card of the day' log refill; 22-day Major Arcana mini study journal; Original card-back pattern sticker sheet

**Risks:** Copyrighted art: never use scans or recolored editions of commercial decks; this product uses no card art at all.; Deck names can be trademarks; keep every deck, publisher and artist name out of the listing and the book.; KDP can reject low-effort or near-duplicate journals; keep the orientation pages original and useful.; Showing commercial deck art in videos is a gray area; film the journal pages instead.; The bundle test depends on the astrology workbook going ahead.

**Validation test:** Bundle with the astrology workbook; track attach rate.

**Continue if:** Raises the bundle's conversion rate. · **Change direction if:** Sell it standalone as a KDP paperback for gifting. · **Stop if:** No lift and no standalone sales after 4 weeks.

## Holiday Mahjong Night Kit (3 designs)

**Trend:** American mahjong boom (Test now) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (88/100)

**Buyer:** Hosts of holiday mahjong nights (Hanukkah, Christmas, New Year's Eve) and gift-givers

### Super prompt

```text
You are a party-stationery designer and a patient mahjong host. Your goal: write and lay out a printable "Holiday Mahjong Night Kit" in three original designs that a host edits, prints at home and uses this December.

BUYER AND JOB: Hosts of holiday mahjong nights (Hanukkah, Christmas, New Year's Eve) and people giving a mahjong set as a gift. Mahjong has become a girls'-night and holiday-hosting favorite; this kit turns a game night into an event and helps first-timers join in without slowing the table.

THREE DESIGNS (identical layouts, different palette and motifs):
A "Festival of Lights" (Hanukkah): Night Blue #1D3557, Candle Gold #E9B949, Silver Mist #D9DEE5, Snow #FAFAF7; motifs: a nine-candle menorah (eight in a row plus one raised helper candle), a dreidel with no letters, small stars.
B "Evergreen" (Christmas): Pine #24523F, Berry #B23A48, Gold #C9A227, Cream #F6F0E3; motifs: pine sprigs, ribbon, a gift box.
C "Midnight" (New Year's Eve): Ink #141414, Champagne #E8D3A2, Silver #BFC5CC, Pearl #F7F5F0; motifs: confetti, streamers, a clock face with no numbers.
Fonts: Playfair Display (headings), Montserrat (body, 10 pt minimum), Great Vibes (names only). White or very light page backgrounds so pages print cheaply.

PIECES (per design; give exact text and layout for each; US Letter landscape sheets, and I will make A4 landscape copies, so keep a 0.25 in safe margin and thin grey cut lines):
1. Invitation, 5x7 in portrait, 2 per sheet: headline, [HOST NAME], [DATE], [TIME], [ADDRESS], [RSVP BY], and "New to mahjong? We'll teach you."
2. Place cards, flat 3.5x4 in folding to 3.5x2 in, 4 per sheet, editable [GUEST NAME].
3. First-timer table tent: a sheet folded in half lengthwise (11x4.25 in faces, one face printed upside down so both sides read): "How a Turn Works" in 5 short steps for American-style play, and a second version for classic Chinese-style tables. Plain, accurate, encouraging.
4. Score pad, half-letter 5.5x8.5 in (A5 for A4), 2 per sheet: table name, date, 4 player columns, 12 game rows, winner, hand value, running totals.
5. Menu card, 5x7 in, 2 per sheet: a cocoa bar and a cocktail-and-mocktail version, each with 4 original drink names and ingredients (at least 2 alcohol-free) and one snack line.
6. Gift sheet: a 3.5x5 in "Lesson Voucher" ("Good for one mahjong lesson with [NAME]") and 6 gift tags at 2x3.5 in, for anyone gifting a set.
7. One instructions page: printing, cutting, folding, editing.

ACCURACY: American turn: draw from the wall (or call the latest discard only to complete an exposure or declare Mah Jongg), add it to your rack, discard one tile face up and name it, play passes to the right; jokers only in groups of three or more, never in a pair or single; match a hand on your current official card to declare Mah Jongg. Classic Chinese-style: most hands are four sets plus a pair. Where rules vary, write "House rules vary." Re-check the menorah description (nine candles).

CONTENT RULES: Original wording, warm and inclusive, about 7th-grade reading level. Mention the card only as "your current official card". Original tile drawings only, shown face-down or with simple dots and bamboo; no Chinese characters drawn by image AI.

DIFFERENTIATION: Score pads alone are common. This kit styles the whole evening in three looks, helps first-timers with table tents, and gives gift-givers a lesson voucher.

CONSTRAINTS: No official card hands, league names or logos, no tile-brand or designer-set names or artwork, no faux-"Asian" novelty fonts or stereotypes, no brand-name drinks or spirits, no song lyrics, no film or TV references, no real people, no health or outcome claims.

OUTPUT FORMAT: Shared text once, then Design A, B and C sheet by sheet as "SHEET n | piece | size and grid | layout | exact text", listing only the lines that change per design (headlines, drink names, motifs). Then a list of illustration assets per design. If you run out of space, stop after a design and wait for me to type "continue".

SELF-CHECK before answering: (1) 3 designs x 7 pieces present; (2) every piece fits its sheet on Letter and A4 landscape with a 0.25 in margin; (3) each menu has at least 2 alcohol-free drinks; (4) turn steps are accurate and say "House rules vary" where needed; (5) no card hands, brand names or stereotyped imagery; (6) all text is original. Fix anything that fails, then answer.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Design A 'Festival of Lights' spot illustrations; ChatGPT images (ask for a transparent PNG)):**

```text
Set of 5 flat gouache-style holiday illustrations arranged in a row on a transparent background: a Hanukkah menorah with exactly nine candles (eight candles in a straight row plus one raised helper candle in the middle), all lit; a dreidel with plain blank sides; a mug of cocoa with marshmallows; a short stack of face-down game tiles with plain ivory backs; a few small scattered stars. Colors night blue #1D3557, candle gold #E9B949, silver mist #D9DEE5. No letters, no characters, no text, no logos, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 3:2, transparent background.
```

Colors pine #24523F, berry #B23A48, gold #C9A227 on cream accents #F6F0E3. No letters, no characters, no text, no logos, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 3:2, transparent background.

```text
Overhead photo of a cozy holiday game-night table: a cream linen tablecloth, a few face-down game tiles with plain backs, two blank white folded place cards, a blank white 5x7 card, a mug of cocoa, small evergreen sprigs and soft fairy-light bokeh, warm evening light, large empty areas for placing printed designs. No text, no characters on tiles, no logos, no brand items, no people, no watermarks. Photorealistic, aspect ratio 4:3. --ar 4:3 --style raw --no text, letters, logos, people, watermark
```

### Build and export

1. Paste the super prompt into Claude or ChatGPT, type 'continue' until all three designs arrive, and save the output in a Google Doc. Have an experienced player read the two 'How a Turn Works' tents.
2. In Canva create one US Letter landscape design (11 x 8.5 in) and build Design A sheet by sheet with guides: 2 x 5x7 invitations, 4 x place cards (3.5 x 4 in flat, dashed fold line), the folded table tent (top face rotated 180 degrees), 2 x score pads (5.5 x 8.5 in), 2 x menu cards, and the voucher-plus-tags sheet. Add thin grey (#BFC5CC, 0.5 pt) cut lines.
3. Generate the three illustration sets; if a tool will not give a transparent PNG, generate on solid white and remove the background (Canva Pro's remover or a free remover). Count the menorah candles (nine) before using it.
4. Duplicate Design A's pages twice and restyle as Design B and C: swap the palette hex codes, illustrations, headlines and drink names; keep layouts identical.
5. Make A4 versions: duplicate at 297 x 210 mm landscape, switch score pads to A5 (148 x 210 mm), and check that every piece still fits inside a 0.25 in margin.
6. Export Share > Download > PDF Print, no crop marks: one Letter PDF and one A4 PDF containing all three designs. Make a 1-page 'Edit your kit' PDF with Canva template links for the invitation, place card and menu pages, tested in a private window.
7. Create 6 listing images at 2700 x 2025 px (all three designs together, one per design, table-tent close-up, 'what's included' list) and a 10-15 second video of printing and folding a place card and tent.
8. Publish on Etsy with the three PDFs (Letter, A4, template links) and on Gumroad; schedule Pinterest pins at 1000 x 1500 px.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my digital download "Holiday Mahjong Night Kit" for people hosting a mahjong night in December and for anyone gifting a mahjong set.
Product facts: three original designs in one download (Festival of Lights for Hanukkah, Evergreen for Christmas, Midnight for New Year's Eve); per design: 5x7 invitations (2 per sheet), fold-over place cards (4 per sheet), first-timer table tent 'How a Turn Works' (American-style and classic Chinese-style versions), score pads (2 per sheet), cocoa-bar and cocktail/mocktail menu cards, lesson voucher card and 6 gift tags, instructions page. Formats: US Letter and A4 PDFs plus editable template links for names, dates and menus (free Canva account). Instant download; print at home or at a copy shop. [ADD YOUR PAGE COUNT]

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters that starts with the buyer's search phrase (choose from "Mahjong Party Printable", "Mahjong Night Invitation", "Holiday Mahjong Party Kit"), then key pieces and the three holidays. Show the character count.
2. 13 TAGS of 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, from phrases like: mahjong party, mahjong invitation, mahjong night, mahjong score sheet, mahjong gift, hanukkah party, christmas game night, new years eve party, game night printable, mahjong place cards, holiday hosting, mahjong lover gift, girls night games.
3. DESCRIPTION: a 2-line opening; "What's included" by piece and design; sizes and file formats; how delivery and editing work; printing tips (cardstock, fold lines); what is not included (tiles; the official card); a line that illustrations were made with AI assistance and designed by me; license (personal use, not for resale); a 5-question FAQ (Can I edit the text? Which holidays? Will it print on A4? Can beginners use the table tent? Can I use it for a birthday?). Add a note to download early for holiday timing.
RULES: No trademarked or brand terms in the title or tags (no league names or abbreviations, no tile-brand or designer-set names, no drink or spirit brands, no software brands); one plain compatibility note about the editing app may appear in the description only. No claims about outcomes. Do not describe mahjong as exotic or mysterious. Output in order: Title, Tags, Description.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Holiday party printables are a core Etsy search category, and buyers expect instant downloads for hosting.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Holiday hosting ideas are planned on Pinterest weeks ahead, so pins posted in late October can surface by early December.
- **Instagram** (traffic): Styled table-setting Reels fit the girls'-night hosting audience.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct link for the email list built by the instructor and club kits, and an easy bonus file for kit bundles.

**Positioning:** A coordinated holiday mahjong night in one download: invitations to score pads in three original looks, first-timer table tents so new players can join in, and a lesson voucher for anyone gifting a set.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Thu Oct 29, 2026):** Publish the $8 listing (input deadline: by Nov 1). Post the first 4 of 10 Pinterest pins (one per design plus the table tent).
- **Day 1 (Fri Oct 30):** Add it to the Gumroad store and as a 'pairs with' line on the instructor and club kit listings; email the mahjong list once.
- **Day 3 (Sun Nov 1):** Post a Reel/TikTok: 'Setting a holiday mahjong table in 30 seconds' with the printed kit. Pin 2 more designs.
- **Day 7 (Thu Nov 5):** Finish the 10 pins. If the listing has favorites, start the optional $1/day Etsy Ads for 14 days.
- **Day 14 (Thu Nov 12):** Post Reel 2: the lesson voucher as a gift idea for people buying a mahjong set. Check views and favorites.
- **Day 21 (Thu Nov 19):** Interim check: if favorites are coming but sales are few, start offering the kit as a bonus inside the club kit now (the input's pivot) while keeping the listing live.
- **Day 27 (Wed Nov 25):** Stop check: under 50 views by Nov 25 means stop promoting it and fold it into the club kit as a bonus.
- **Day 35 (Thu Dec 3):** Last push: 'print it tonight' pins and a Story for early-December Hanukkah hosting (verify the 2026 dates) and Christmas parties; run a 25% sale through Dec 24.
- **Day 42 (Thu Dec 10):** Continue check: at least 5 sales by Dec 10 means keep it, add a New Year's Eve push and plan a respectful Lunar New Year 2027 edition. Fewer sales: keep it only as a bundle bonus.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Seasonal pins per design and per piece on a 'Holiday mahjong night' board, linking to Etsy. | 4 pins on Day 0, 10 by Day 7, then 1 per day through Dec 10 |
| Instagram Reels / TikTok | Table-setting and printing videos; the voucher as a gift-with-a-set idea. | 2 per week Nov 1-Dec 15 |
| Etsy Ads | $1/day once favorites appear. | Daily for 14 days (Nov 5-18), optional |
| Email (Gumroad list from the mahjong kits) | Holiday hosting email with the kit and a December sale code. | 1 email at launch, 1 in early December |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** mahjong party printable, mahjong night invitation, mahjong score sheet, holiday game night printable, hanukkah party printable, christmas game night, new years eve party printable, mahjong gift, mahjong place cards, girls night game ideas

**Hooks:**
- Hosting mahjong night this December? Print the whole table in one go.
- New player at the table? This tent card explains a turn in 5 steps.
- Three looks: Festival of Lights, Evergreen, Midnight.
- Gifting a mahjong set? Add a 'one lesson with me' voucher.
- Invitations, place cards, score pads and a cocoa menu that match.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $8.00 (range $6.00–$12.00, estimate). $8 is the input's test price: inside the $1-12 band of existing mahjong printables but with three full coordinated designs and first-timer tents, so it reads as good value against a single score pad. Use a 25% Etsy sale in December rather than a lower list price.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $8.00 | $1.21 | $0.00 | **$6.79** | 85% |
| Gumroad | $8.00 | $1.30 | $0.00 | **$6.70** | 84% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $14.20; break-even 3 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 8 hours; answer in about 42 days.
- **What you risk:** About 8 hours and $0-14 in ads. The risk is timing: if Etsy does not surface the listing before mid-December, the season ends before the test does.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost; reusable every holiday season and adaptable to birthdays and Lunar New Year; strengthens the mahjong product line as a bundle bonus even if it sells little alone.
- **Bad bet if:** You cannot list it by Nov 1, or Etsy page 1 for 'mahjong party printable' is already full of matching kits under $5. It is the least asymmetric of the three mahjong products: seasonal with a hard end date and in a crowded printable category.

**Ways to extend:** Lunar New Year 2027 edition designed respectfully, ideally with an Asian-American collaborator or reviewer; Birthday, bachelorette and Galentine's mahjong night editions from the same templates; Spring 'new card night' party kit (no card content); Bonus file inside the club and instructor kits (also the pivot); Editable 'mahjong night' invitation-only listing at $3-4 as a low-price entry product

**Risks:** Hard seasonal deadline: sales stop after December; a late listing may not be indexed in time.; Image AI can draw a seven-branch menorah or garbled Hebrew letters on a dreidel; check every illustration before use.; Mixing holiday motifs with mahjong must stay respectful; avoid stereotyped 'Oriental' styling.; Score pads and invitations are crowded on Etsy; the table tents and voucher are what make it different, so feature them in photos.; Keep official card hands, league names and tile-brand artwork out of every piece.

**Validation test:** List by Nov 1 at $8; 10 Pinterest pins; watch favorites and sales through Dec 15.

**Continue if:** At least 5 sales by Dec 10. · **Change direction if:** Favorites but few sales: bundle it into the club kit as a bonus. · **Stop if:** Under 50 views by Nov 25.

## Six Poems Broadside Set: Letterpress-Style Public-Domain Poetry Prints

**Trend:** Poetcore (Watch) · **Format:** poster (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (88/100)

**Buyer:** Book lovers and Poetcore/dark-academia fans decorating reading nooks or dorms; gift buyers

### Super prompt

```text
You are a typographer and literary editor who designs letterpress-style broadsides. Produce a curated set of six public-domain poem broadsides for a Poetcore audience.

BUYER AND JOB
Book lovers and Poetcore / dark-academia fans decorating reading nooks, desks and dorms, plus gift buyers. Poems and books are core props of the look; they want wall art that feels like a sheet from an old printing house, not a generic quote poster.

THE SIX POEMS (each first published in English before 1930 and in the US public domain)
1. Emily Dickinson, "Hope is the thing with feathers": the text as printed in Poems, Second Series (1891), titled "Hope". Do not use later scholarly editions; their edited texts may still be under copyright.
2. John Keats, "Bright Star" ("Bright star, would I were stedfast as thou art"), a 19th-century printed text (first published 1838).
3. Christina Rossetti, "Remember", from Goblin Market and Other Poems (1862).
4. Walt Whitman, "A Noiseless Patient Spider", Leaves of Grass text (1881-82 edition).
5. Elizabeth Barrett Browning, Sonnet 43 ("How do I love thee? Let me count the ways"), Sonnets from the Portuguese (1850).
6. W. B. Yeats, "He Wishes for the Cloths of Heaven", The Wind Among the Reeds (1899).

ACCURACY
Reproduce every word, line break, stanza break, capital and punctuation mark from the named edition. Give the line count. If you are not certain of any word or mark, put [VERIFY] at the end of that line instead of guessing; the seller will check against a scan. Do not modernize spelling ("stedfast" stays). Never add, merge, trim or "improve" lines.

DELIVERABLE SPEC
Master layout per poem at 18 x 24 in (3:4), then specs for 24 x 36 in (2:3), 16 x 20 in (4:5), 11 x 14 in and ISO A2 (420 x 594 mm), all at 300 DPI. Layout: aged-paper background; ornamental rule at top; title in letter-spaced small caps; poem set flush left in a centered text block no wider than 60% of the sheet; poet name and life dates below; one original ink-blot ornament; a small colophon at the foot, e.g. "Text from Poems, Second Series, 1891. Public domain." Margins at least 9% of the short side. Give point sizes per ratio so the longest poem fits without crowding and no line wraps.
Fonts: Cormorant Garamond (poem), Cormorant SC or Playfair Display SC (titles), IM Fell English if available. Palette "Iron Gall": Aged Paper #F3EBDD, Iron Gall Ink #1F1B16, Oxblood #6B1E1E, Faded Ink Blue #2F3E57, Sepia #8A6F4D. Text in Iron Gall Ink; one accent color per sheet. Optional subtle letterpress impression (inner shadow under 10% opacity).

CONTENT REQUIREMENTS
Also write: a set title; a 40-word original note per poem for the listing (facts only if certain: publication year and collection; no invented biography or interpretation presented as fact); a pin headline per poem under 60 characters. Tone: quiet, literary, plain English.

DIFFERENTIATION
Curated, accurately transcribed texts with real typographic craft and a printed source line, versus generic quote posters with misattributed or altered lines.

CONSTRAINTS
Public-domain texts only; original layout and ornaments; no poet portraits or likenesses; no modern poets, translations, song lyrics, film, TV or celebrity references.

OUTPUT FORMAT
For each poem: (a) title, poet, life dates, source edition and year; (b) the full text in a code block exactly as it should be typeset; (c) line count; (d) layout notes per ratio with point sizes; (e) colophon line; (f) listing note and pin headline. Then describe a 2 x 3 grid "set preview" image.
Self-check, fixing failures before answering: [ ] six poems, each first published before 1930; [ ] Dickinson text from the 1891 printing; [ ] line counts match the texts; [ ] uncertain words marked [VERIFY]; [ ] no portraits, translations or modern references; [ ] five ratios specified.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Aged paper background texture for all six broadsides; Midjourney):**

```text
antique laid paper texture, warm cream #F3EBDD with faint chain lines, gentle foxing spots near the edges, soft uneven deckled edges, flat even scan lighting, clean center area, no folds --ar 3:4 --style raw --no text, letters, logos, watermark, people, trademarked characters (upscale to at least 5400 px on the long side; crop for the other ratios)
```

**Image prompt 2 (Original ink-blot and ornament set; ChatGPT images):**

```text
Set of 6 original ink ornaments on a transparent background, 1:1, 2048 x 2048 px, even grid: a small symmetrical ink blot, a splatter with three droplets, a quill-stroke flourish, a simple fleuron, a horizontal ornamental rule, a tiny ink drip. Iron Gall Ink #1F1B16 with slightly translucent edges, hand-printed feel. No text, no letters, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Room mockup for a single framed broadside; ChatGPT images):**

```text
Photorealistic interior, 4:5 portrait: a warm off-white plaster wall above an old wooden desk with stacked cloth-bound books with blank spines, a brass candle holder, a folded tweed scarf on a chair and a leather satchel. On the wall, a thin dark wood frame (3:4) with a cream mat and blank paper inside for overlay. Soft afternoon window light, Oxblood #6B1E1E and Sepia #8A6F4D tones. No people, no readable text, no book titles, no logos, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 4 (Gallery-wall mockup for the set of six; ChatGPT images):**

```text
Photorealistic reading nook, 4:3 landscape: six matching thin black frames (3:4) hung in a 2 x 3 grid above a worn leather armchair with a wool throw and a stack of old books with blank spines; each frame holds blank Aged Paper #F3EBDD paper for overlay; wall in warm off-white, chair in Oxblood #6B1E1E. Soft lamp light. No people, no readable text, no logos, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Verify each poem word for word against a scan of the named edition (Internet Archive, HathiTrust or Project Gutenberg); resolve every [VERIFY]; record the edition, year and scan URL for each poem in a Google Sheet as your rights log.
2. Canva: create a custom 18 x 24 in master; place the upscaled paper texture, set the type per the AI spec, add the ornament and colophon; duplicate for all six poems so the set matches.
3. Make the other ratios (24 x 36 in, 16 x 20 in, 11 x 14 in, A2 420 x 594 mm) with Resize (Canva Pro) or by duplicating into new custom sizes; recheck margins and confirm no poem line wraps.
4. Export each ratio as one 6-page PDF Print file (one poem per page, 300 DPI). If you also offer JPGs, check their pixel dimensions and state the honest maximum print size in the listing.
5. Package five files for Etsy (one per ratio), each under the 20 MB limit (verify). If any is too large, deliver a 1-page PDF with a cloud-folder link instead.
6. Mockups: place the finished art into the frame mockups in Canva; make a 2 x 3 set image, one close-up per poem and a size-guide image (at least 2000 px wide).
7. Pinterest: make one 1000 x 1500 px pin per poem plus one set pin, each linking to the Etsy listing.
8. Redbubble (secondary): upload each poem as its own work at the highest resolution you have; enable posters, art prints and greeting cards; set your markup.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for a printable set of six letterpress-style poetry broadsides.
Product facts: poems by Emily Dickinson ("Hope is the thing with feathers", 1891 text), John Keats ("Bright Star"), Christina Rossetti ("Remember"), Walt Whitman ("A Noiseless Patient Spider"), Elizabeth Barrett Browning (Sonnet 43) and W. B. Yeats ("He Wishes for the Cloths of Heaven"); every poem first published before 1930 and in the US public domain, transcribed from early printed editions with a source line on each print. Files: five PDFs, one per ratio (2:3 up to 24x36 in, 3:4 up to 18x24 in, 4:5 up to 16x20 in, 11x14 in, ISO A-series up to A2), 300 DPI. Instant download; no physical item.
Produce:
1. A title under 140 characters starting with "Poetry Print Set of 6" or "Literary Wall Art Printable", then poetcore, dark academia decor, book lover gift. Show the count.
2. Exactly 13 tags of 20 characters or fewer, with counts (ideas: poetcore, poetry print, literary wall art, dark academia, poem print set, book lover gift, reading nook decor, emily dickinson, keats poem, vintage poem print).
3. A description: hook; the six poems with poets and years; sizes and formats; how delivery works (download from Etsy Purchases; print at home or at a print shop; frame not included); printing tips (matte or textured paper, print at 100%); a 5-question FAQ: Are the poems public domain? (Yes, all first published before 1930; each print shows its source edition.) Can I buy one poem? [YES/NO]. Will colors match my screen? (Close; paper and printer vary.) Personal or commercial use? (Personal use only.) Refunds? [YOUR DIGITAL-FILE POLICY].
4. Alt text for 6 images.
Rules: no modern poets, song lyrics, film, TV or celebrity references; no brand names (frame, paper or print-shop brands); no claims about mood or wellbeing; poet names only as accurate attribution.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Printable wall art and book-lover gifts are bought here; zero marginal cost.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Poetcore and literary-decor are visual, saveable searches; the input's test is built on pins.
- **Redbubble** (secondary): Physical posters and art prints for buyers who don't want to print, with no inventory.
- **TikTok and Instagram Reels** (traffic): Slideshow 'poems for your poetcore nook' posts suit the fashion-driven audience.

**Positioning:** Poetcore is a look; these are the poems that go with it: six public-domain classics, source-checked and typeset like a 19th-century broadside.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Suggested Mon Oct 12, 2026. Run the super prompt and verify all six poems against scans; start the rights log.
- **Day 2:** Build all ratios, export, make mockups; list the set on Etsy at $9 and upload to Redbubble.
- **Day 3:** Create a Pinterest board ('Poetcore Reading Nook') and publish pins 1-5 (set pin plus four poems).
- **Day 7:** Pins 6-10; first TikTok/Reel slideshow: '6 public-domain poems for your poetcore reading nook'.
- **Day 14:** Pins 11-15; compare saves per pin and note which poem leads.
- **Day 21:** Pins 16-20 focused on the leading poem; second Reel.
- **Day 30:** Wed Nov 11, 4 weeks after listing: continue if saves and favorites are steady and a few sales have come; pivot if saves but no sales (make one poem a free pin download and push the commonplace book instead); stop if no traction after 4 weeks and 20 pins.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | One pin per poem plus set and mockup pins, tagged with poetcore and literary-decor phrases | 5 pins per week (20 in 4 weeks) |
| TikTok / Instagram Reels | Slideshows of the prints in a styled nook; caption with the poem's first line | 1 per week |
| Etsy | Swap the thumbnail to the best-saved poem | Weekly |
| Redbubble | Passive; tag each work accurately | Set up once |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** poetcore, poetry wall art, literary print set, dark academia decor, poem print, book lover gift, reading nook decor, vintage poem print, public domain poetry, romantic poem print

**Hooks:**
- Poetcore is a look. These are the poems that go with it.
- Six poems old enough to be free, typeset like 1890.
- Your reading nook needs a broadside, not a quote poster.
- Dickinson as first printed in 1891, checked line by line.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $9.00 (range $6.00–$14.00, estimate; competition was not measured in the research). Digital literary print sets commonly sit in the single digits to low teens (background knowledge, unverified); $9 for six curated, source-checked prints undercuts physical prints while signaling more craft than single quote posters.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $9.00 | $1.31 | $0.00 | **$7.70** | 86% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 8 hours; answer in about 30 days.
- **What you risk:** About 8 hours and $0 cash. The answer comes in about 4 weeks.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost and rights-free text; thousands of public-domain poems make an endless series; sells on Etsy and Redbubble with Pinterest traffic; evergreen with seasonal peaks (Valentine's, April).
- **Bad bet if:** Literary prints turn out saturated with no premium for the Poetcore framing (competition was not measured), Pinterest traffic is too slow to show signal in 4 weeks, or transcription errors slip through and earn bad reviews.

**Ways to extend:** 'Ink Night' dark colorway of the same six; Single-poem listings at a lower price; Seasonal sets: autumn (Keats, 'To Autumn'), winter, Valentine's love poems, April for National Poetry Month; Greeting-card versions of each broadside; Bundle with the Commonplace Book journal; 'Pick your poem' from a pre-verified public-domain list

**Risks:** Transcription errors from the AI; every poem must be checked against a scan; Edition rights: later scholarly editions (notably of Dickinson) can still be under copyright; use only the named early printings; Competition in literary wall art was not measured and is likely heavy; Poetcore fashion coverage may drop after autumn 2026; Canva image exports may not reach 300 DPI at the largest sizes; state the true maximum size; AI textures can carry artifacts or fake letters; inspect at 100% zoom

**Validation test:** List the set as a digital download plus 10 pins tagged poetcore/literary decor; compare saves against your other boards.

**Continue if:** Saves and favorites are steady and a few sales come within 4 weeks. · **Change direction if:** Saves but no sales: make one poem free as a pin magnet and sell the journal instead. · **Stop if:** No traction after 4 weeks and 20 pins.

## 2027 First-Time Reader Plan (New Testament, Minimal + Soft Editions)

**Trend:** Gen Z Bible-reading revival (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (87/100)

**Buyer:** Adults aged 18-35 who are new to reading the Bible, including first-time Bible buyers; separate design for the young-men segment

### Super prompt

```text
You are an editor of beginner reference material who is precise with Bible references and neutral across Christian traditions. Your goal: write the complete content for "2027 First-Time Reader Plan", a references-only plan for reading the New Testament in 2027, in two visual editions.

BUYER AND JOB
Adults 18-35 new to reading the Bible, often first-time Bible owners. Job: a simple schedule and plain facts about how the Bible is organized, without being told what to believe. One edition is minimal (designed with young men in mind), one soft.

PLAN STRUCTURE (standard, verifiable)
The 27 New Testament books in canonical order, 260 chapters, one chapter each weekday (Monday-Friday) from Monday January 4 to Friday December 31, 2027: exactly 260 weekdays. Weekends are open for catch-up. Friday January 1 is "Orientation day" (read pages 2-6). Chapter counts: Matthew 28, Mark 16, Luke 24, John 21, Acts 28, Romans 16, 1 Corinthians 16, 2 Corinthians 13, Galatians 6, Ephesians 6, Philippians 4, Colossians 4, 1 Thessalonians 5, 2 Thessalonians 3, 1 Timothy 6, 2 Timothy 4, Titus 3, Philemon 1, Hebrews 13, James 5, 1 Peter 5, 2 Peter 3, 1 John 5, 2 John 1, 3 John 1, Jude 1, Revelation 22.
Anchors: Matthew 1 = Mon Jan 4; Mark 1 = Thu Feb 11; Acts 1 = Fri May 7; Romans 1 = Wed Jun 16; Revelation 1 = Thu Dec 2; Revelation 22 = Fri Dec 31.

DELIVERABLE (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4)
1 Cover: "2027 First-Time Reader Plan" / "The New Testament, one chapter a weekday" / "[YOUR SHOP NAME]".
2 How This Plan Works: one chapter a weekday; weekends free; missed a day? pick up at today's reading or use the weekend; works with any translation; references only.
3 How the Bible Is Organized: two main parts, the Old Testament and the New Testament; traditions differ in which books they include (most Protestant Bibles have 66 books, Catholic Bibles 73, Eastern Orthodox Bibles more), while the 27-book New Testament is shared by all three; Jewish scripture (the Tanakh) contains the same books as the Protestant Old Testament, arranged differently.
4 How to Read a Reference: book, chapter, verse ("John 3:16"); ranges ("Mark 1:1-8"); numbered books ("1 John" is not "John"); chapter and verse numbers were added long after the books were written, to help readers find passages.
5 The New Testament by Type: Gospels (Matthew, Mark, Luke, John); History (Acts); Letters (21: thirteen open with Paul's name; the others are Hebrews, James, 1-2 Peter, 1-3 John, Jude); Apocalyptic (Revelation). Table: book | chapters | one neutral, factual line.
6 Old Testament at a Glance: genre groups (Law, History, Poetry and Wisdom, Prophets) in the 66-book Protestant order, labeled as such, noting that other traditions include and order books differently.
7 Year Tracker: 27 books with one box per chapter (260 boxes).
8-19 Monthly pages, January-December: table Date | Day | Reading | check box; weekend rows shaded "Open / catch-up".
20 Notes: date | reference | notes.
21-22 Undated Version for late starters: Weeks 1-52 x Mon-Fri readings.

EDITIONS (same text)
Minimal: Bone #EDEBE6, Charcoal #232323, Olive #5E6B4E, Slate #6B7A89, Rust #A0522D; Oswald headings, Inter body; square grids, no florals.
Soft: Cream #FBF7F0, Dusty Rose #D8A7A0, Sage #A8B5A0, Warm Taupe #8C7B6B, Ink #3A3A3A; Cormorant Garamond headings, Lato body; thin botanical line accents.

CONTENT RULES
References only: no verse text from any translation. Original, plain, descriptive wording at about an 8th-grade level. Say "traditionally" or "many readers" where traditions differ; never explain what a passage means or take sides on doctrine or authorship.

WHY THIS IS BETTER
A first-timer's pace that fits real weeks, orientation pages most trackers skip, and a minimal edition for buyers who don't want florals.

CONSTRAINTS
No ministry, church, app, publisher or translation brand names; no names of published reading plans; no claims about spiritual, emotional or mental-health outcomes.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Page by page: "=== PAGE [n]: [title] ===", "Layout:" (one line), exact text, monthly tables as pipe tables. End with:
SELF-CHECK (fix any "no"): 260 dated readings; each book's chapters consecutive and in canonical order; all six anchors match; no weekend readings; no verse text; no brand names; no outcome claims; wording neutral and original.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Minimal edition cover and pin background; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Minimal geometric composition: a simple line drawing of an open book seen from above, drawn with single charcoal #232323 lines, and a thin olive #5E6B4E horizontal rule beneath it. Bone background #EDEBE6. Lots of empty space at the top for a title added in Canva. Flat, clean, masculine-minimal, no florals. Aspect ratio 3:4 (at least 2550x3400 px). Text: none. No readable writing on the pages, no crosses or other religious symbols (keeps it neutral). No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

Aspect ratio 3:4 (at least 2550x3400 px). Text: none. No religious symbols. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.

Soft daylight; the page flat and square to the camera so the real plan can be placed over it in Canva. Aspect ratio 4:5. Text: none, no title on the book. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt. If any self-check item fails, ask the AI to regenerate the monthly tables.
2. Verify the schedule yourself: 260 readings in total; the six anchors (Matthew 1 Mon Jan 4, Mark 1 Thu Feb 11, Acts 1 Fri May 7, Romans 1 Wed Jun 16, Revelation 1 Thu Dec 2, Revelation 22 Fri Dec 31); chapter counts per book against any printed Bible's table of contents; no weekend readings.
3. Accuracy and neutrality review (the rights note asks for one): have one or two people who read the Bible, ideally from different traditions, read pages 2-6 and flag anything inaccurate or one-sided. Fix it before designing.
4. In Canva, create a US Letter design (8.5 x 11 in) for the Minimal edition (Oswald/Inter, Minimal palette) and build the monthly page with Elements > Table (Date | Day | Reading | check). Paste in the content.
5. Duplicate the design for the Soft edition: swap to Cormorant Garamond/Lato, the Soft palette and thin botanical accents.
6. Export each edition as PDF Print (300 DPI) in Letter, then Resize (paid plan) or copy into A4 designs and export: 4 PDFs in total.
7. Create two separate Etsy listings (Minimal, Soft) at the same price with the same number of photos for the style test; on Gumroad, sell one product with both editions.
8. Make 3 pins per edition with Canva's Pinterest Pin template (1000x1500) and export as PNG.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my printable "2027 First-Time Reader Plan - [MINIMAL or SOFT] Edition": a references-only plan to read the New Testament in 2027 at one chapter per weekday (Monday Jan 4 to Friday Dec 31, 2027; weekends open for catch-up), with orientation pages (how the Bible is organized, how to read a reference, the New Testament by type, Old Testament at a glance), a 260-box year tracker, 12 monthly checklists, a notes page and an undated weekly version for late starters. 22 pages; US Letter and A4 PDFs. Buyer: adults 18-35 who are new to reading the Bible. [For the Minimal edition add: clean design, no florals.]

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase (for example "Bible Reading Plan 2027" or "New Testament Reading Plan 2027"), then key features. Give 3 options and mark your pick.
2. 13 TAGS, each 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, covering bible reading plan, 2027, new testament, bible tracker, beginner, printable, and minimalist or aesthetic (match the edition).
3. DESCRIPTION: two-line opener; "What's inside" bullets; "Files and sizes"; "How delivery works" (instant download from Etsy Purchases; nothing ships; print at home); "Good to know" (references only, no scripture text; works with any translation; the 27-book New Testament is shared by Catholic, Orthodox and Protestant Bibles); one line saying AI tools helped draft it and the orientation pages were checked for accuracy; usage terms ([YOUR CHOICE: personal use only, or small groups may print copies]).
4. FAQ with 5 Q&As: Which translation do I need? Is any Bible text included? What if I start late or miss days? Can I print copies for my study group? Refunds (answer: [YOUR REFUND POLICY]).

Rules: no ministry, church, app, publisher, translation or reading-plan brand names (for example, never the names of Bible apps or devotional brands, and never the phrase "One Year Bible"); no claims about spiritual growth, peace, healing or mental health; neutral across Christian traditions; original wording; plain US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Bible reading trackers already sell there, and two listings make the style test easy to read in Etsy Stats.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct link for Instagram and Pinterest with both editions in one download.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Faith printables and reading plans are popular pins, and January-start plans are searched ahead of time.
- **Instagram** (traffic): Factual carousels (how the Bible is organized) suit the young-adult buyer named in the research.

**Positioning:** A first-time reader's plan for 2027: the New Testament at one chapter a weekday, plain orientation pages, and a minimal edition for people who don't want florals.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Nov 16, 2026):** Run the super prompt; verify the 260 readings and the six anchors.
- **Day 2 (Wed Nov 18):** Send pages 2-6 to your reviewers for accuracy and neutrality, and fix what they flag.
- **Day 3 (Thu Nov 19):** Build both editions in Canva, export 4 PDFs, and make the mockups and 6 pins.
- **Day 4 (Fri Nov 20):** List both editions as separate Etsy listings (same price, same photo count); publish one Gumroad product with both.
- **Day 7 (Mon Nov 23):** Instagram carousel "How the Bible is organized, in 5 slides" built from pages 3-5, link in bio to Gumroad.
- **Day 29 (Tue Dec 15):** Continue window opens: every Monday until Jan 10, record favorites, visits and sales per edition.
- **Day 34 (Sun Dec 20):** Style read: compare favorites and clicks by edition.
- **Day 49 (Mon Jan 4, 2027):** "Day 1 is today" pin and post; point late starters to the undated weekly pages.
- **Day 55 (Sun Jan 10, 2027):** CONTINUE if there were sales, or favorites grew week over week, between Dec 15 and Jan 10. PIVOT if only one style moves: drop the other and add the matching journal or a Bible-tab printable in the winning style.
- **Day 60 (Fri Jan 15, 2027):** STOP if there are no sales.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Plan and orientation-page pins, split evenly between editions, linking to each edition's Etsy listing. | 5 pins at launch, then 3 a week |
| Instagram | Factual carousels (how the Bible is organized, how to read a reference, New Testament by type) and a plan flip-through. | 2 posts a week |
| TikTok (optional) | "260 chapters, 260 weekdays" explainer and a printed-plan setup video. | 1-2 a week if you have time |
| Christian subreddits and Facebook groups | Many ban selling; offer the free orientation page only where allowed. Check the community rules. | Occasional, only where allowed |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** bible reading plan, 2027 bible plan, new testament plan, bible tracker, bible reading chart, bible plan for men, minimalist bible, first time bible, bible study printable, bible plan printable

**Hooks:**
- Never read the Bible before? Start with one chapter a weekday.
- 260 chapters, 260 weekdays: the New Testament fits exactly into 2027.
- Weekends off for catch-up.
- How the Bible is organized, on one page.
- A reading plan without the florals.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $5.00 (range $3.00–$8.00, estimate: anchored on the $1.87 Etsy tracker example in the research; no broader price survey). Trackers sell as low as $1.87 and free plans are everywhere, so stay under the impulse line; the orientation pages, 2027 dating and undated backup justify a small premium over bare trackers.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $5.00 | $0.93 | $0.00 | **$4.08** | 82% |
| Gumroad | $5.00 | $1.00 | $0.00 | **$4.00** | 80% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 10 hours; answer in about 60 days.
- **What you risk:** About 10 hours plus a reviewer's time, and $0.40 in listing fees. Style answer by Dec 20; continue or stop by Jan 10-15.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost. The structure rebuilds for 2028 in about an hour (same chapters, new dates), the undated version sells in any month including Lent/Easter and back-to-school, and a matching journal and tabs make a series. The revival data covers several years, so the trend should outlast this launch.
- **Bad bet if:** The price ceiling is low ($2 trackers and free church or app plans are everywhere), so it only matters at volume. If reviewers find neutrality problems you can't fix, or the community reacts badly to commercial products, stop. Honestly: cheap to try, modest upside.

**Ways to extend:** Matching undated reading-notes journal (the next product); Bible book tabs printable (book names only); Psalms and Proverbs add-on plan (references only); Undated evergreen version as its own listing; 2028 edition (same chapters, new dates); 40-day Lent and Easter plan for the secondary peak; Small-group leader pack with neutral discussion templates

**Risks:** Free plans from churches and apps are a strong substitute.; Accuracy and neutrality: one wrong reference or one-sided sentence costs trust; the review step is not optional.; Some buyers may see commercial faith products as inauthentic; keep the copy plain and practical.; Never add verse text from modern translations; if a verse is ever quoted, use World English Bible text (KJV is public domain in the US but under Crown rights in the UK).; Keep app, ministry and published-plan names out of tags and titles.; At $5, Etsy fees take about a fifth of each sale (estimate).

**Validation test:** List both styles by Nov 20 and pin weekly. Watch which style earns more favorites and clicks by Dec 20.

**Continue if:** Sales, or favorites growing week over week, during Dec 15 to Jan 10. · **Change direction if:** Only one style moves: drop the other and add a matching journal or Bible-tab printable in the winning style. · **Stop if:** No sales by Jan 15, 2027.

## Race-Day Pacing Kit: Split Calculator Sheet + Wrist Strips

**Trend:** Hyrox-style fitness racing (Prepare) · **Format:** template (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (87/100)

**Buyer:** First-time racers 2-6 weeks out

### Super prompt

```text
You are a spreadsheet designer and printable-stationery designer. Produce a complete race-day pacing kit for first-time racers of a standardized 8-station indoor fitness race (eight 1 km runs, each followed by one workout station). The kit does arithmetic on the racer's own numbers; it never tells them how fast to go.

BUYER AND JOB
First-time racers 2-6 weeks out. They have a target finish time and some practice times and want a concrete split plan, a card to carry and a checklist that keeps race week calm. Book bonuses and apps exist; this is a cheap, single-purpose, race-week kit that complements them.

PART 1: GOOGLE SHEETS FILE (give every tab, cell, label, formula, number format, validation and protected range)
Tab "Start Here": title, 6-step instructions (make a copy, fill yellow cells, print the strip), the disclaimer.
Tab "My Times":
- A2 "Target finish time", B2 input, format [h]:mm:ss. A3 "Scale factor", B3 =IF(D23=0,"",B2/D23), format 0.000. C2 =IF(B3="","",IF(ROUND((G21-B2)*86400,0)=0,"Adds up","Check inputs")).
- Row 5 headers: # | Segment | Type | My practice time | Share | Target time | Target clock at end | vs practice.
- Rows 6-21, 16 segments in order: Run 1, Ski machine, Run 2, Sled push, Run 3, Sled pull, Run 4, Burpee broad jumps, Run 5, Rowing machine, Run 6, Farmers carry, Run 7, Sandbag lunges, Run 8, Wall balls. Row 22 "Transitions (total)". D23 =SUM(D6:D22).
- E6 =IF($B$3="","",D6/$D$23) (percent); F6 =IF($B$3="","",D6*$B$3) (m:ss); G6 =IF($B$3="","",SUM($F$6:F6)+$F$22/16*(ROW()-5)) ([h]:mm:ss); H6 =IF($B$3="","",IF(F6>=D6,"+","-")&TEXT(ABS(F6-D6),"m:ss")). Fill E6:H6 down to row 21. F22 =IF($B$3="","",D22*$B$3).
- Validation on B2 and D6:D22: duration greater than 0:00:00. Inputs filled #FFF4C2. Grey italic example inputs labelled "EXAMPLE: replace with your own times".
Tab "Wrist Card": print-ready block pulling segment names, target times and clock from "My Times" (show formulas); two columns (runs | stations); Space Mono 12 pt; print setup landscape, fit to width, no gridlines.
Tab "Race-Day Actuals": same 16 segments plus transitions; target | actual (input) | difference (signed m:ss) | running total; total row.
Tab "Checklist": checkboxes; sections Clothing and shoes, Ticket/ID, Bag, Before the start, "My food and drink plan (what / when)" as blank rows only, After the finish, "Event rules to check" (gloves, headphones, bag drop).

PART 2: PRINTABLE PDF (US Letter and A4 versions)
Page 1 Wrist strips 10 x 2 in (A4: 265 x 48 mm), 4 per landscape page, two rows of 8 boxes (top Run 1-8, bottom the stations in order), blank time lines, dashed cut lines.
Page 2 Mini pace cards 3.5 x 2 in (A4: 85 x 55 mm), 10 per portrait page, 16 segments with blank target lines.
Page 3 One-page race-week checklist matching the tab.
Page 4 "How to fill in your strip" with a worked example marked EXAMPLE, plus the disclaimer.

DESIGN
Palette "Chalk & Signal": Chalk #F7F7F5, Graphite #1E2328, Signal Orange #FF5A1F, Steel Blue #3A6EA5, Concrete #9AA3AB. Fonts: Oswald headings, Inter body, Space Mono for times. Header rows Graphite with Chalk text; only input cells yellow.

CONTENT RULES
Original wording, grade 7 reading level. No pacing, training, nutrition, hydration or medical advice; no promises about finish times. Never name any race series, organizer, sponsor, equipment maker or apparel brand; generic station names only.

DISCLAIMER (verbatim): "This kit only does arithmetic on the times you enter. It is not coaching, nutrition or medical advice. Not affiliated with or endorsed by any race organizer."

OUTPUT FORMAT
Tab by tab, a cell table (cell | content or formula | format). Then the PDF page by page: dimensions, grid and every label.
Self-check, fixing failures before answering: [ ] with the example inputs, G21 equals B2 and C2 reads "Adds up"; [ ] every formula points at the right rows; [ ] 8 runs and 8 stations in race order everywhere; [ ] Letter and A4 sizes both given; [ ] no brand names; [ ] no advice or outcome claims.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing thumbnail / title card; Ideogram):**

```text
Bold graphic title card, 4:3 landscape, 2400 x 1800 px. Render this text exactly: "RACE-DAY PACING KIT" in heavy condensed sans-serif capitals, and below it smaller: "Split calculator · Wrist strips · Checklist". Solid Chalk #F7F7F5 background with three Signal Orange #FF5A1F horizontal split lines and small Graphite #1E2328 tick marks like a timing strip, Steel Blue #3A6EA5 accent dot. Flat vector, clean. No people, no logos, no brand names, no trademarked characters, no watermarks, no other text.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Flat-lay mockup for the printable strips; ChatGPT images):**

```text
Photorealistic top-down flat-lay, 1:1, 2048 px: a printed paper wrist strip with blank boxes, a small blank pace card, two safety pins, small scissors and a roll of athletic tape on a light concrete surface. All paper blank for overlay in Canva. Soft daylight, crisp shadows, Signal Orange #FF5A1F tape as the only color accent. No people, no hands, no logos, no text, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Create a new Google Sheet and build the tabs from the AI output. Type each row-6 formula once, then drag it down to row 21. Set formats with Format > Number > Custom number format: [h]:mm:ss for clock cells, m:ss for segment cells.
2. Test it: enter a 1:30:00 target and the example practice times; confirm C2 reads 'Adds up' and G21 equals B2. Clear B2 and confirm no error messages appear.
3. Protect formula ranges (Data > Protect sheets and ranges > Show a warning) and set the Wrist Card print setup to landscape, fit to width, gridlines off.
4. Share > General access: Anyone with the link, Viewer. Copy the link and replace everything from '/edit' onward with '/copy' so buyers get a 'Make a copy' screen; test it in a private browser window.
5. Canva: make a 1-page US Letter 'Your sheet link' PDF with the /copy link on a big button. Build the wrist strips at 11 x 8.5 in (Letter landscape) and 297 x 210 mm (A4 landscape), the mini cards and the checklist; export each set as PDF Print.
6. Prepare the Etsy files: link PDF, Letter printables PDF, A4 printables PDF, each under 20 MB (verify Etsy's limit).
7. Listing images (at least 2000 px wide): Sheet screenshot with EXAMPLE data, strip mockup, mini cards, checklist preview, 'what's included' graphic.
8. List on Etsy at $5, mirror on Gumroad, and add a cross-link in the training log listing.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for a digital race-day pacing kit for first-time racers of an 8-station indoor fitness race (eight 1 km runs plus eight workout stations).
Product facts: a Google Sheets split calculator delivered as a "make a copy" link inside a PDF (needs a free Google account); it scales the buyer's own practice times to their target finish time and shows a target time and race clock for every run and station; printable wrist strips and mini pace cards (US Letter and A4 PDFs); a race-week checklist. Instant download; nothing is shipped.
Produce:
1. A title under 140 characters starting with the buyer's phrase (e.g. "Race Pace Calculator Google Sheets" or "Fitness Race Pacing Kit"), then wrist pace strip, race day checklist. Show the count.
2. Exactly 13 generic tags of 20 characters or fewer, with counts.
3. A description: hook; what's included; file formats and sizes (Google Sheets link, PDF, Letter 8.5x11 in and A4); how delivery works (download the PDF from Etsy Purchases, click the link, choose Make a copy; print strips at 100% scale); a 5-question FAQ: Do I need Excel? (No, free Google Sheets.) Will it tell me what pace to run? (No, it only does arithmetic on the times you enter.) Which races does it fit? (Any race with eight 1 km runs and eight stations; not affiliated with any organizer.) Can I use it on my phone? (Yes, in the Google Sheets app; printing works best from a computer.) Refunds? [YOUR DIGITAL-FILE POLICY].
4. Alt text for 4 images.
Rules: never use the name, logo or slogan of any race series, organizer, sponsor, equipment maker or apparel brand anywhere. Do not write "official". No pacing, nutrition or medical advice and no promises about finish times. Plain US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Same shop and buyers as the log; cross-selling the two is the main lever.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct link for bios and the free lead-magnet pivot (pay-what-you-want with email capture).
- **Instagram Reels** (traffic): A screen recording of 'type goal time, get 16 splits' is a quick, visual demo.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Checklist and pace-strip pins pick up race-prep searches over time.

**Positioning:** Type your goal time, get every split: a $5 race-week kit that turns your own practice times into a carry-on plan.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Suggested Wed Oct 21, 2026, the same week as the log. Build and test the Sheet; list on Etsy at $5 and cross-link with the log.
- **Day 1:** Gumroad listing; put the link in your bio.
- **Day 2:** Reel/TikTok: screen recording of entering a target time and watching the splits fill in.
- **Day 5:** 3 Pinterest pins: Sheet screenshot, wrist strip sheet, checklist.
- **Day 8:** Video: printing, cutting and taping a wrist strip; no people on camera needed.
- **Day 11:** Create the log + kit bundle listing at about $10.
- **Day 14:** Review views and clicks; replace the first photo if views are coming but clicks are not.
- **Day 21:** Wed Nov 11 check: continue if at least 5 sales; pivot to a free lead magnet for the logbook (Gumroad, email capture) if views come without sales; stop if under 75 views in 21 days.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Instagram Reels | Screen-recorded demos and the strip-cutting video | 1 per week, alternating with log videos |
| TikTok | Same demos, captioned 'first 8-station race?' | 1 per week |
| Pinterest | Checklist and strip pins linking to Etsy | 2 pins per week |
| Etsy | Cross-link with the log; bundle listing | Weekly stats check |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** race pace calculator, split calculator, pace band printable, race day checklist, wrist pace card, fitness race pacing, google sheets pace, race split planner, hybrid race pacing, race week planner

**Hooks:**
- Type your goal time. Get all 16 splits.
- The wrist strip you cut out the night before race day.
- Race-week brain is real. Here's the checklist.
- Your practice times, scaled to your goal: no guessing at math mid-race.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $5.00 (range $4.00–$8.00, estimate; competitor prices were not measured in the research). The input's $5 test price: a single-purpose impulse add-on below the $7 log; bundle both at about $10 so the kit lifts the order value. It competes with free calculators, so it must stay cheap.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $5.00 | $0.93 | $0.00 | **$4.08** | 82% |
| Gumroad | $5.00 | $1.00 | $0.00 | **$4.00** | 80% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 6 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 6 hours and $0. The answer comes in 21 days.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost; works as an upsell, a bundle sweetener or a free lead magnet, so it adds value even if it sells poorly alone; reusable every race season.
- **Bad bet if:** Free calculators and official apps already cover pacing for this audience, or the log gets no traffic for it to ride on. On its own this is a small add-on, not a strong asymmetric bet.

**Ways to extend:** Bundle with the Split & Station Log; Doubles/relay version that splits stations between partners; Generic version for other timed running races (5K/10K pace bands); Free lite version (strip only) as an email lead magnet; Post-race 'actuals vs target' recap template for social sharing

**Risks:** A formula error would damage trust; test before listing and after every edit; 'Make a copy' links can be shared freely, so some piracy is likely; Copy or examples could read as pacing advice; keep everything arithmetic-only; Brand wording slipping into titles, tags or videos invites takedowns; Very low price means small dollars even if it converts

**Validation test:** List at $5 alongside the log; measure views and sales over 21 days.

**Continue if:** At least 5 sales in 21 days. · **Change direction if:** Offer it as a free lead magnet for the logbook. · **Stop if:** Under 75 views in 21 days.

## Phone-Free Table Kit (Friendsgiving + Winter/NYE editions)

**Trend:** Analog bag & screen-free gifting (Test now) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (86/100)

**Buyer:** Friendsgiving and holiday-dinner hosts aged 18-35

### Super prompt

```text
You are a party-game designer and a relaxed, generous holiday host. Your goal: produce the complete content of a printable "Phone-Free Table Kit" in two editions, Friendsgiving and Christmas/New Year's Eve, that a host downloads, prints at home and uses the same night.

BUYER AND JOB: Friendsgiving and holiday-dinner hosts aged 18-35. Their friends say time together matters more than presents, and phone-free events are growing; hosts want an easy, kind way to get phones off the table and keep conversation going, without lecturing anyone.

DELIVERABLE (each edition; US Letter 8.5x11 in, and I will make A4 copies, so keep a 0.25 in safe margin; ink-light white backgrounds with color accents):
1. Phone-basket sign, 5x7 in, 2 per landscape sheet: "Phones rest here tonight" plus an editable line such as "Back after dessert". Also a basket label, 4x2 in.
2. 60 conversation cards, 2.5x3.5 in, 9 per sheet (3x3), 7 sheets (the last 3 slots are blank "Write your own" cards) plus one card-back sheet for double-sided printing. Three decks of 20: Warm-Up (easy and funny), Story Time (memories and favorites), What If (imagination). 45 cards are shared by both editions and 15 are seasonal (5 per deck).
3. Four original pen-and-paper party games, each with a one-page rules card (half-letter 5.5x8.5 in) and a score or play sheet if needed: (a) a write-draw-write paper telephone game; (b) a timed letter-and-category listing game; (c) an anonymous "guess who wrote it" answers game; (d) a fold-over drawing game (head, body, legs). Give each an original name and complete rules: players, time, materials, setup, how to play, scoring, and variations for 4 players and for 12 or more.
4. Place cards: tents 3.5x2 in folded (3.5x4 in flat), 4 per sheet, editable name on the front and a "question for your neighbor" on the back.
5. Host note, 5x7 in: three short versions (playful, warm, a text message to send guests beforehand) that explain the phone basket in a friendly way.
6. Instructions page: printing, cutting, and a 3-step evening flow (arrival: basket; dinner: cards; dessert: games).

DESIGN: Friendsgiving "Harvest Table": Cream #FBF5EA, Pumpkin #D9772B, Cranberry #9E2B3A, Sage #8DA47E, Bark #4A3B2F. Winter/NYE "Candlelight": Snow #F8F8F5, Midnight #1E2A44, Gold #C9A44C, Evergreen #2F5D50, Berry #A23B4D. Fonts: DM Serif Display (headings), Nunito (body, 10 pt minimum), Caveat (accents). Give layout notes for each sheet: grid, card front and back layout, icon spots.

CONTENT RULES: Original wording; warm, playful, never preachy or anti-technology; reading level grade 6-8; gender-neutral; works for mixed ages 13+ and for guests who do not celebrate the holiday. Cards avoid politics, religion, money, bodies, dating status, trauma and heavy confessions; each can be answered by anyone in under 2 minutes. No claims about health, wellbeing, "detox" or relationship outcomes.

DIFFERENTIATION: Phone-free is the hook, which generic Friendsgiving trivia and invitations do not address; signage, cards, games and place cards form one coordinated system; place cards carry neighbor questions; two editions share one design system.

CONSTRAINTS: Do not use or allude to trademarked game names (for example Pictionary, Scattergories, Telestrations, Taboo, Codenames, Mad Libs, Heads Up) or their exact formats or card layouts; no phone or app brands; no named clubs, events or creators; no song lyrics, film or TV references; no real people.

OUTPUT FORMAT: Edition 1 (Friendsgiving), then Edition 2 (Winter/NYE), sheet by sheet as "SHEET n | piece | size and grid | layout | exact text". Write the 45 shared cards once, then each edition's 15 seasonal cards and seasonal sign and host-note lines. If you run out of space, stop after Edition 1 and wait for me to type "continue".

SELF-CHECK before answering: 60 cards per edition (45 shared + 15 seasonal), 20 per deck, no duplicates or near-duplicates, no banned topics; 4 games with complete, playable rules and original names; 9 cards fit a Letter sheet (7.5x10.5 in) and an A4 sheet (190.5x266.7 mm); no brand or trademarked game names; no claims. Fix anything that fails, then answer.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Friendsgiving 'Harvest Table' spot illustrations; ChatGPT images (ask for a transparent PNG)):**

```text
Set of 6 flat hand-drawn illustrations in a row on a transparent background: a woven basket holding three plain face-down phones (blank backs, no logos, no camera bumps or brand shapes), a pie with a lattice top, two autumn leaves, a lit taper candle, a pencil on folded paper, a small gravy boat. Colors pumpkin #D9772B, cranberry #9E2B3A, sage #8DA47E with bark #4A3B2F outlines. No text, no letters, no logos, no people, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 3:2, transparent background.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Winter/NYE 'Candlelight' spot illustrations; ChatGPT images (ask for a transparent PNG)):**

```text
Set of 6 flat hand-drawn illustrations in a row on a transparent background: a woven basket holding three plain face-down phones (blank backs, no logos or brand shapes), a strand of string lights, a lit candle, falling confetti, a round clock face with hands at twelve and no numbers, a pair of mittens. Colors midnight #1E2A44, gold #C9A44C, evergreen #2F5D50, berry #A23B4D. No text, no letters, no logos, no people, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 3:2, transparent background.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Conversation card back pattern (one per edition, recolor in Canva); Ideogram (or ChatGPT images)):**

```text
Seamless pattern of small speech-bubble outlines, tiny stars and dots, flat line style, pumpkin #D9772B and sage #8DA47E lines on solid cream #FBF5EA, evenly spaced, calm. No text, no letters, no logos, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 5:7, tileable edges.
```
